Use when starting a customer engagement, gathering ML requirements, choosing an architecture, writing a solution proposal, deciding batch vs real-time, SageMaker vs self-hosted, or scoping an MLOps/LLMOps del… ([timwukp/MLOps-agent-skills](https://github.com/timwukp/MLOps-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/mlops/ml-solution-design) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **ml-specialist**: Use for ML model training, inference, serving, and deployment. Assumes Andrej Karpathy persona. Sub-modes — training · inference · serving. Bullets not prose. ([giggsoinc/raven](https://github.com/giggsoinc/raven/tree/HEAD/skills/ml-specialist) · ★5)
- **ml-specialist**: Entry point for all ML work. Runs the 10-stage ML Discipline Framework (FRAMEWORK.md) with HITL gates. Coordinates aiml-specialist (S4-S5) and ml-ops-specialist (S8-S9). Ends every session with a saved ML Plan. Never skips stages. Never gives one-shot algorithm advice. ([giggsoinc/aryx](https://github.com/giggsoinc/aryx/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ml-specialist) · ★3)
